# TA98 digestive duct review

## Cystic fold construction, 10 October 2026

The complete authority inventory below still applies. The current male cystic source is the installed finite FJ3080 wall; the female source is LOCAL:female:cystic_duct:authored. Both represent A05.8.02.011. Its child A05.8.02.012 (Plica spiralis / spiral fold) lacks geometry in both. The existing related arterial and neural sources do not represent their small duct-wall branches.

Construct three separate oblique crescentic mucosal projections rooted in the actual cystic lumen boundary. Each fold is a finite tissue volume with a basal footprint shared with the duct, a raised luminal surface and tapered edges. One collective .012 mesh may contain three disconnected folds, as the term describes a series. No duplicated exposed basal faces, lumen disks, additional whole-duct surface, organ relocation or new image texture. Conforming subdivision is limited to the cystic footprint support; retain gallbladder/trunk mouths, all original coordinates and unrelated anatomy. The cystic parent selects its wall and folds; the .012 child selects the folds alone. Standard selection, visibility, labels and continuous section caps must work.

Reference: Pina et al., 46 adult human extrahepatic tracts, https://www.thieme-connect.com/products/ejournals/html/10.1055/s-0035-1567879 . The study describes separate oblique/transverse endoluminal folds, variable number and extension, and no mandatory continuous helix. This construction represents that morphology, retaining the TA98 name. It does not copy patient geometry. Projection is limited by the existing local lumen radius and leaves a central passage. Verify each fold's components, closed finite volume, opposed basal contacts, self-intersections, complete macro-lumen continuity, inherited mouth coordinates/normals and actual longitudinal/cross-sectional renders before local installation. The dimensions are illustration parameters, not measurements of these two subjects.

All 124 authority descendants of liver, gallbladder, pancreas and duodenum are retained below. The initial inventory preceded geometry changes; later construction and delivery evidence is recorded below. A whole-organ exterior does not represent sinusoids, lobules or segmental vascular territories. Openings are not solids. An authored provisional tissue interval is a representation, not anatomical acceptance.

## Source routes and adjoining interfaces

Female native gallbladder/bile-duct communication is a reciprocal association, with the cystic duct explicitly not separately represented. Filling and emptying are bidirectional; this is not a one-way serial branch. The native branched bile-duct mesh remains unpartitioned; its source name does not prove exact hepatic, cystic and common-duct region extents. Its route reaches the native whole duodenum with the major papillary region shown as unmodeled text. Whole duodenal exterior does not establish a separately represented descending portion, papillary opening or wall tissue.

Male main/accessory pancreatic ducts lead to the source-qualified descending duodenal partition, through the major/minor papillary regions respectively. The accessory route is conditional on patency, and neither a universal accessory duct nor a universal shared hepatopancreatic ampulla is asserted. Male common-hepatic/bile-trunk regions link only within their actual shared FJ3079 source. Cross-source cystic, hepatic and duodenal interfaces remain unresolved; no independently positioned donor is inserted into these paths.

TA98 chapter ancestry of bile/cystic ducts remains, but these adjoining pathways are excluded from false physical containment within gallbladder wall tissue. Female Show components is disabled when no independently represented gallbladder tissue regions exist, while the related-structures action remains enabled. Male original gallbladder tissue regions remain eligible. Geometry, normals, UVs and material arrays are untouched; no source partition, new opening solid or copied reference geometry is created.

Sources: [Biliary duct anatomy](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/books/NBK459246/) and [Accessory pancreatic duct specimens and termination variants](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4413057/). These establish named routes and variants, not native geometry acceptance. Evidence: `digestive-duct-native-relations.json` and `tests/digestive-duct-native-paths.test.mjs`.

## Runtime verification and limits

Live female gallbladder inspector now shows one reciprocal duct association, text-only cystic intermediate and disabled Show components; false Contains/part-of duct links are gone. Showing related anatomy preserves byte-identical physical camera position, target, up, quaternion and projection. The actual native gallbladder and branched bile-duct appearance was inspected after a separate explicit Center.

Live male accessory duct inspector now distinguishes Santorini from Wirsung using a source-qualified description that takes priority over the mismatched fetched main-duct summary. Minor papillary drainage and its variants remain explicit. Related expansion preserves byte-identical physical camera pose/projection. Actual source duct/descending duodenal material and standard labels were inspected after explicit Center; inherited coarse/ragged partition boundaries remain visible and unaccepted. Do not infer a patent papillary ostium from this rendering.

Six duct regressions plus hepatic and camera checks pass (11 tests in the final combined run). The complete 7,474-term selectors/descendant sets in eight catalogues, component exclusion checks and TypeScript pass. Production build passes in a clean temporary output directory; the existing large bundle remains a delivery limitation. Both runtime JSON hashes remain unchanged. No added meshes, triangles, textures, draw groups or anatomically accepted terms. UI evidence: `digestive-duct-runtime-ui.json`; screenshots: `outputs/female-gallbladder-runtime-relations.jpg`, `outputs/male-accessory-pancreatic-duct-relations.jpg`.


## Native gallbladder wall construction — 9 October 2026

The nineteen authority terms above remain the full obligation. Serosa (.006) is a regional peritoneal covering, absent at the hepatic attachment; subserosa (.007), muscular layer (.008) and mucosa (.009) are finite tissue intervals. Mucosal folds (.010) and spiral fold (.012) require luminal relief, not extra full-body coats. Cystic and bile ducts (.011/.013) are adjoining conduits, not components inside gallbladder tissue; their openings are boundary/interface geometry. Sphincters (.014–.016/.018) are local muscular specializations. The ampulla (.017) is a regional lumen/wall junction, not a plug, and glands (.019) are microscopic structures. Do not add a gastric muscularis mucosae or submucosa to gallbladder. Histological basis: [NCBI gallbladder anatomy](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/sites/books/NBK459288/).

The runtime review also preserves the local candidate mode in copied links. Derived peel-cover lists now use actual rendered visibility and opacity, so an invisible isolated cystic duct is not offered as a covering to remove. This does not remove anatomical geometry. The previous full explosion-viewport suite passed sixteen checks; no camera/explosion geometry changed in this hierarchy update.

Before the next geometry edit, repair the complete common-hepatic/fork carrier rather than pinning its artificial upper cut. The existing authoritative inventory and three-domain assembly/interface definitions remain applicable. Stage a single paired Loop subdivision field over the native source exterior topology and its corresponding inner field. Apply identical subdivision stencils to both fields, preserving their wall correspondence. Taper only transverse displacement over Y=1.1978–1.210 m, limit it to 0.45 mm, and retain every axial coordinate, cystic seam, Ga cell, fold, pancreatic course and superior branch beyond the support exactly. Transfer the field by source-triangle barycentric coordinates to the actual repaired carrier, retaining the bounded former-opening crown. Conformingly refine only affected actual faces; rebuild the upper tissue annulus from the resulting literal cut curves, not a lumen disk. The complete tissue surface and both adjoining domains must pass continuous paired thickness, topology, self-crossing, Float32 and whole-forest checks and actual external/cut rendering before choosing this trial. This is a morphology repair candidate; it does not establish clinical calibre or named tributary allocation. Keep prior candidates and both ordinary atlases unchanged until review supports installation.

The first fork trial passes complete finite-cell topology/self-crossing and continuous source-wall thickness with a uniform field factor 0.5 (maximum actual movement 0.225 mm). Float32 full-context replay finds nine new liver exterior faces and four new liver/inner-wall faces; no other primary neighbor adds faces. Installation is rejected. Before another deformation, constrain the same paired field using actual native liver-surface clearance: pin both corresponding fields where either lies within 0.5 mm of that surface, then smoothly admit the field over 0.5–1.0 mm clearance. Do not move, delete or hide liver tissue and do not treat inner-wall penetration as normal hepatic attachment. Keep the previous unconstrained candidate/report. Whole-body replay and actual render must determine whether this constraint yields an adequate repair; clearance weighting is not itself proof of disjointness or anatomical acceptance.

The liver-bound trial is archived as `fork-liver-bound.npz`, SHA256 `542295b0b9e51230b270e4632a5cb62076c3e9b90f26001152754388c37dbc2f`. It pins 198 paired field controls at the liver boundary; 397 original carrier controls move, by at most 0.225 mm. All axial coordinates and original controls beyond the support remain literal. It adds 1,188 vertices / 2,376 triangles only to this uninstalled trial, with no UV/image runtime assets. Continuous paired source-field Jacobians remain negative. An independent Float32 replay re-triangulates the actual rounded annuli and confirms all three closed finite domains and the complete wall have zero tested self-crossings (`common-hepatic-fork-liver-bound-precision.json`). This replay supplements the generator and does not certify clinical anatomy.

Against all 178 actual forest neighbors, new primary inner-wall contacts decrease 4→0 and outer contacts 9→2. The remaining new faces are liver faces 4087 and 4090; ordinary source contacts remain unresolved. The installation gate therefore still fails. Existing Blender renders preserve prior scenes and do not save the main file. Actual close-up shows a less faceted stem, but the pointed upper contour and residual former-opening crease remain; added topology does not yet justify runtime replacement. Keep both trials and their failure evidence. Do not repeat broad bounded smoothing as an anatomical acceptance strategy: distinguish the retained fork transition from the repaired former-cystic-mouth patch, and repair their actual contours/correspondence before declaring a named interval complete. Ordinary female `a362a09…`, ordinary male `ef928b80…` and the previously visible local common-hepatic prototype remain unchanged. The four current hierarchy/interface/catalogue checks pass; no new distinct accepted TA98 coverage is claimed.

Actual exterior review still shows an angular superior flare and a crease near the repaired former cystic mouth. A reproducible twenty-section source scan (`measure-common-hepatic-sections.py --scan`, `common-hepatic-transition-scan.json`) locates lumen expansion from 1.79 mm² at Y=1.198825 m to 2.41 mm² at Y=1.199723 m and 3.39 mm² at Y=1.200172 m, with the former opening's rim spanning Y=1.199960–1.202204 m. These source observations distinguish the affected region; they do not establish clinical dimensions or justify moving the named boundary arbitrarily to conceal a defect. Complete carrier/rim repair and hepatic-fork allocation must be reviewed together before ordinary installation. Clinical calibre, native innermost vessel contacts, hepatic tributary identities and distal papillary allocation remain unfinished. This local candidate is not anatomically accepted, and the whole-body TA98 goal remains active.

Whole-assembly review distinguishes two issues: the horizontal hepatic daughter begins below the first source-Y slice that separates into two contours, so the earlier first-two-loops plane is not a verified common-hepatic anatomical limit; the former cystic sidewall patch separately retains a tangent mismatch at its fixed rim. Before editing again, repair only that patch's four existing paired radial rings with cubic Hermite interpolation. Derive rim tangents from the actual adjoining native exterior faces, exclude the removed opening's old fan, and use the same tangent planes and radial correspondence for both wall fields. Retain the current paired crown/hubs, every rim coordinate and all axial coordinates; match a common tangent plane at the centre. This changes no faces, image/UV assets, named boundaries or adjoining anatomy. Verify continuous paired patch thickness, complete wall and finite-domain self-crossings, runtime precision, forest neighbors and actual rendering. Defer boundary repartition until whole-wall morphology supports it; do not treat contour-count onset as a clinical branch junction or lower a cut merely to hide the existing defect.

The rim-normal replay shows nearly horizontal adjoining tangent planes. Holding every interior ring's Y coordinate fixed makes a finite tangent projection impossible there; the first candidate stops before producing geometry. Amend only the patch interior constraint: allow its transverse and axial Hermite corrections, capped by a 0.25 mm axial-change gate, while every rim, hub, named cut curve and coordinate outside the patch remains literal. Project derivatives onto the actual three-dimensional tangent planes instead of dividing by a nearly zero transverse normal. No new anatomical boundary or body-placement change is authorized by this amendment.

### Native gastric venous source repair plan — 11 October 2026

The six actual duct-inner/left-gastric-source crossing segments occupy X=−18.918…−18.425 mm, Y=1194.984…1195.355 mm and Z=25.845…26.089 mm. The source has three components, 38 tested self-crossings, four exactly zero-area faces and three four-edge openings. The metadata generator ignored authored TA98 tags and did not read the nested ordinary local catalogues before older source snapshots. Generic names consequently misidentified the provisional duodenal superior part, patellar anterior surface, fibular head and fibular neck. No anatomical surface is changed by this correction.

2026-10-13 terminal-only repair before geometry: `reports/alimentary-terminal-repair-plan-v95.json`, SHA256 `47d64debef0913a3be5c8c58fc9715924a32622c1816730e73a3d4dd41d25e77`, inherits the complete149-term checked inventory and fixed body/forest requirements. Reject enlarged v94 despite regular walls: its native-guided profile worsens actual vascular, pancreatic, node and caval contacts. Preserve the installed LOCAL male D calibre and original stations0-18, with at most0.1micrometre common-bank float32 rebase. Transport only six terminal sections (maximum30mm endpoint displacement) through a smooth tangent cubic/RMF and an explicit matched-profile transition into all nine literal J inlet fields. J/I, skeleton and all context stay fixed. Compare complete wall/surface and all active neighboring anatomy against baseline; actual review precedes any provisional local improvement. The gastric-D, ileocaecal and anal gaps remain separate obligations. No whole anatomical acceptance or deployment.

2026-10-13 source-bound coupled D-J root correction before geometry: `reports/coupled-duodenojejunal-plan-v96.json`, SHA256 `d124df3d9dbb29dbe6e1fb15928b22486e434e377296ac88d3c44860469b99d6`, retains the checked149-term inventory and fixed skeleton/aorta/context. Actual existing J rim vertices are within0.6mm of the aorta; independent D attachment worsens contact. Carry the shared nine-interface root14mm leftward (positiveX), derived from actual aortic maxX and2mm collar reserve, with first30mm of J moved rigidly and a180mm smooth source-arc transition. Preserve section shape/thickness, all unmodified J/I and D upstream anatomy; allow at most50mm D endpoint-control motion required by the relocated true collar. Compare10/12mm anterior guide bulges through all wall/self/paired-sheet and actual body/functional gates; no new J/I fault may be accepted. Existing clinical/source wall and other tract defects remain explicit. No local promotion until verified improvement and actual rendered review; no public deployment.

2026-10-13 fixed-port terminal course correction before geometry: `reports/native-duodenal-clearance-plan-v98.json`, SHA256 `2fc8734e96b998be0a6f691e576e4b4491423ce21b8a869683789cf857ec79e0`, retains all149 terms and fixed source/forest obligations. Reject v96 root translation: it introduces131 exterior/121 lining crossings between nearby J loops, despite locally valid wall cells. Restore every original J/I field and the original true inlet. Mere0.12mm surface proximity did not prove an unavoidable penetration. Compare10/14mm anterior and0/−6mm inferior bounded smooth guide offsets for the native six terminal D sections, within the existing30mm total control-motion bound. Require complete D/whole junction self/wall and actual all-body/functional checks plus Blender/section review before any local promotion. No radius shrink, independent coat change, skeleton/context movement, anatomical acceptance or deployment.

### Bounded medial ileal loop trial · v99

The complete current 149-term inventory above remains the source/ownership inventory. The v98 D-J junction passed its tissue and joined-surface checks but still crosses the aorta/SMA and is **not accepted or installed**; its exact wall and fixed source context were inspected in the existing Blender.

Before moving any tissue, plan `reports/bowel-medial-loop-plan-v99.json` freezes the existing v86 J-I bank (SHA256 `9f6e5be1a1b4404e29720e91de2998c83243c52a45481f7fb499355eea499a58`). One spatially smooth whole-section translation of at most 12 mm medially and 4 mm inferiorly addresses the superior lateral ileal lobe crossing the liver and ascending colon. Each original ring and all nine interfaces move together; all owner faces, thicknesses, original control counts and physical inlet/outlet collars remain. The native skeleton, D, colon, vessels and all other context stay fixed. This trial is not a new partition or representation of any missing TA98 term. No added invalid cells, no outer/lining self-crossings and comparative actual-body clearance plus Blender cross-section review are required before promotion.

The translation-only v99 trial was rejected before body screening: it adds invalid tissue prisms despite preserving each profile. The next permitted correction rotates each displaced section by the minimum proper rotation from its old to its new course tangent, using the same bounded centres. Every interface shares this rigid transform; unmodified course intervals remain literal, no radius scaling. The same no-new-fault and body/render acceptance gates apply.

The rigid-section local-redirection helper and hard per-span translation allocation are now implemented in the reusable `geometry/` Python tools; all **23** analytic/tool tests pass. The bounded v99 loop trial retains exact ports, source calibre and thickness, adds zero invalid tissue prisms, and has zero complete outer/lining self-crossings. Its **actual fixed-body screen nevertheless fails**: whole-liver outer crossings 147 → 150 and lumen crossings 140 → 144; ascending-colon serosal contacts 195 → 221. It is rejected and not installed. The full 12.65 mm trial also fails its tissue intervals. Both ordinary local model catalogues remain unchanged. Existing Blender contains the exact v98 D-J wall with fixed aorta/SMA/IVC guides; front and side renders confirm that this junction trial is not body-clearance acceptable. No claim of a complete alimentary lumen is made.

### Coupled whole ileal loop fitting · v100

Before changing geometry, frozen plan `reports/bowel-coupled-loop-plan-v100.json` (SHA256 `669e0fc498f915d04a51c9c927b03dcd717d1db8db6dd1bfef9d0b7ce73ec8b2`) binds current ordinary catalogue, v86 source bank and actual fixed colonic/liver facets. The current complete 149-term inventory and source ownership remain applicable. All nine ileal wall interfaces receive the same smooth cubic-spline section translations; no vertices/faces are added, radius or wall thickness scaled, or term invented. Four whole sections at each end stay literal, so the shared J-I collar and current caecal entry remain. Native skeleton, fascia and all surrounding anatomy stay fixed. The solver must retain at least 5% of the negative source prism Jacobian throughout wall depth, cap motion at 75 mm, and recheck actual whole-packet/jejunal contacts. The initial contact directions are below actual liver facets and medial to actual ascending-colon facets. The stage is limited to five searches/60 seconds; saved-array, whole-body, render/section and source measure checks remain mandatory. No anatomical acceptance or installation is authorized by numerical success alone.

### Coupled neighbouring J-I loop trial · v102

The bounded v100 trial clears actual liver crossings 147 → 0 and retains zero faulty ileal tissue prisms, but its moved ileum collides with fixed jejunal/ileal neighbours. It is not installed. Before further change, plan `reports/bowel-coupled-loop-plan-v102.json` (SHA256 `76b099ef0bf57dcb627637489621204b258f70bc085b9040022ff5490b89488f`) freezes the same current 149-term/source inventory and complete joined J-I forest. Two smooth section-translation fields now include neighbouring jejunal sections; all nine interfaces stay together, first/last and shared J-I collars stay literal, and the skeleton/all fixed context remain. Every previously healthy J or I tissue prism must retain negative Jacobian; existing J faults remain explicit and may not worsen. No partition, scaling or invented TA98 geometry is allowed. Physical proximity supplies a fairness term, not permission to merge adjacent loops or treat vessels/nerves as luminal tissue. The source calibre, all actual crossings, shared norms, body contacts and render/section checks still gate installation; the stage is limited to 90 seconds/five searches.

### Compact joined bowel packet trial · v104
